2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 聯準會上周三決議升息一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，FOMC 政策聲明顯示，18 名
FOMC 成員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次 ， 另有4 人認為存在升息兩次的可
能性 ，加上聯準會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)會後表達控制通膨的偏鷹立場 ， 帶動美
元指數走升至 100 高位。
⚫ 歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數
據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解 、 能源價格持續高位情況下 ， 市場對於歐元區經
濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度。
⚫ 美國公布初請失業金人數為 19.6 萬人，優於市場預期的 20.8 萬，再度呼應聯準
會主席華許(Kevin Warsh)認為目前勞動力市場仍具韌性的說法， 在美國經濟穩
健及通膨頑固的情況下，預計聯準會將持續升息，有利美元持穩。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委暨葡萄牙央行總裁佩雷拉(Alvaro Santos Pereira)表示，歐元區目前

通膨雖未廣泛擴散 ， 惟考量近期天然氣價格飆升 ， 央行將密切關注未來通膨壓力
擴散情形。最新利率期貨市場顯示，歐洲央行今年底仍有再升息一碼空間。
⚫ 日本政府通過消費稅減稅及家庭現金補貼的政策大綱，預計自明年 4 月起的兩
年內，將食品消費稅率自 8%降至 1%，惟大綱中未明確說明資金來源，再度引
發市場對於日本政府的財政擔憂，短線不利日圓表現。
⚫ 台灣央行 9/17 宣布重貼現率連 10 凍維持在 2%不變，央行總裁楊金龍表示，台
灣的通膨相較其他主要經濟體溫和，採取漸進緊縮貨幣政策有助物價穩定。 此
外，目前通膨率為 2%，明年有望降至 2%以下，後續將觀察通膨發展、主要經
濟體貨幣政策緊縮程度及台灣金融情勢，以決定未來貨幣政策走向。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.650，低點在 31.958，以 31.808 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 1 角 7 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶 ， 美國CPI 數據公布後加強市場對於聯準會 9 月升息的預期 ，
早盤台幣跳空開低，外資連續三日賣超台股並匯出，加以出口商態度轉為惜售，令
台幣接連貶破 31.700、31.800、31.900 關卡， 週四由於聯準會偏鷹派升息，美元指
數走升至 100.300，早盤台幣跳空開低，台幣最低貶至31.958，不過隨後出口商賣壓
出籠，外資雙向進出，台幣收復部分跌幅，維持在31.900 附近震盪；臨近周末，外
資買超台股並於外匯市場匯入 ， 台幣走升至31.722， 最終在央行調節之下收在31.808。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場而言 ， 美國最新初領失業救濟金人數較預
期減少一萬多人、續領失業救濟金人數亦下降，顯示美國勞動力市場尚屬穩健，加
以聯準會升息抗通膨，鷹派立場給予美元指數支撐、不利台幣表現；就台灣市場來
看，九月台灣央行並未升息，台美利差擴大預計將帶給台幣壓力，此外，儘管聯準
會升息利空出盡，外資買超台股帶動台幣走升，惟台灣即將迎來中秋連假，預計金
融市場交投謹慎，將令台幣陷入橫盤整理格局，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間
震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/21/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。人民幣中間價連續走升，接連升破 6.7700、6.7600 整數
關口，升至三年七個月來高點，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美元指數震盪走強，加
以市場靜待聯準會利率決議公布 ， 交投謹慎 ， 人民幣升幅有限 ， 大致維持高檔震盪 ，
週四聯準會鷹派升息一碼，惟人民幣中間價連續五日走升至 6.7521，並創下四年高
點，帶動人民幣緩步走升，USD/CNH 升破 6.7000 整數大關，終場境外人民幣收在
6.6944，週線升值 94 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6984，週線升值 21 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪走升 。 就國際市場來看 ，FED 最新政策聲明和經濟預測顯
示，聯準會為明年繼續收緊貨幣政策敞開了大門，美元指數走升，抑制人民幣升值
的速度；就中國國內市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實
質拋匯需求將支撐人民幣表現； 最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 匯價維持
在五日均線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，加以USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.7000 整數關
卡，顯示 USD/CNH 空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪走升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6700-6.7200

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走貶。 週初，美國通膨數據維持高位 ，令聯準會升息預期升溫 ，USD/JPY
自 153.29 低點反彈至 155 附近；週間 ，美國聯準會宣布升息一碼 ， 帶動USD/JPY 上
漲至 156.41；臨近週末，日本央行如市場預期升息一碼，惟央行偏鴿派立場令日圓
續貶，帶動 USD/JPY 一度最高來到 158.05，而隨後在日本政府於外匯市場進行匯率
詢價下，USD/JPY 回吐部分漲幅，終場收在 156.86，週線上漲 349 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本政府上周通過消費稅減
稅及家庭現金補貼政策大綱，惟大綱中未明確說明財源，再度引發市場對於日本政
府財政惡化的擔憂，預計將不利日圓走勢。二、日本央行上周五決議將政策利率調
升一碼至 1.25%， 9 名決策委員中有 2 人對升息持反對意見，降低市場對日本央行
進一步升息的預期。另一方面 ， 聯準會上周三亦升息一碼至3.75%-4.00%區間 ，FOMC
政策聲明顯示，18 位官員中有 12 人預計今年將再升息一次，另有 4 人認為存在升
息兩次的可能性，聯準會升息立場明顯較日本央行更為鷹派，預計將支撐USD/JPY
走勢。三、日本財務大臣片山皋月近期再度重申，將於必要時採取外匯干預行動，
以維護外匯市場有序運行。四、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 短中天期均
線向下發散走勢偏空，惟近期匯價在多次向下測試 154 均拉回，明顯下方有買盤支
撐。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：154.80-159.50 
前週交易區間 
153.29 158.05

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/21/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，聯準會升息預期升溫加上歐元區 9 月經濟景氣指
數下滑，令歐元走跌至 1.1525；週間，美國聯準會鷹派升息立場帶動美元走升，歐
元最低來到 1.1460；臨近週末，歐元持續偏弱整理，終場收在 1.1483，週線下跌 107
點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、 歐洲央行管委卡薩克斯
(Martins Kazaks) 表示 ， 為避免美伊戰爭引發的燃料成本上漲開始傳導至工資和其他
商品價格，歐洲央行可能需要漸進式調升利率以抑制通膨。最新利率期貨市場顯示
歐洲央行今年仍有升息一碼的空間，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國聯準會決議將
利率調升一碼至 3.75%-4.00%區間，聯準會主席華許會後偏鷹言論帶動美元指數走
升至 100 上方，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、歐元區 9 月 ZEW 經濟景氣指數自
前月的 31.4 降至 25.8，低於預估的 39.9。數據下滑顯示在美伊衝突未獲緩解、能源
價格持續高位情況下，市場對於歐元區經濟前景抱持較不樂觀的態度，將不利歐元
表現。四、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 近期回測年線未能有效突破後明顯
拉回轉為下行走勢，目前匯價承壓於各天期均線下方，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1350-1.1590 
前週交易區間 
1.1450 1.1599

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.14-115.09.18 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.70% 
公債－10 年期-A15106 1.92 % ~ 1.921% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.86% ~ 1.861%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週央行理監事會議決議結果 ， 維持政策利率不變 ， 符合
市場預期 ， 且近兩週央行持續減發定存單 ， 令市場短錢充裕 ， 惟週五銀行間分
配略為不均，部分銀行有資金需求，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.70%，利率高位較上週上升 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投清淡，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三等殖系統成交在 1.86%，10 年
期指標券 A15106 則未成交，最近成交利率為 1.92~1.921%。 
台灣央行理監事會議如市場預期維持政策利率不變 ， 總裁楊金龍表示 ， 今年國
內通膨仍屬可控，明年有望回落至 2%以下，市場對今年升息疑慮有所降溫，
對台債形成支撐 ， 惟國際債市走勢仍偏空 ， 預期台債殖利率短線仍呈高檔區間
盤整格局，後續關注下週三財政部公布的第四季公債發行計劃，預估 5 年券
區間為 1.79%~1.83%，10 年券區間為 1.90%~1.95%。

## Document 075: 2026年9月29日國際經濟金融週報

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月29日國際經濟金融週報.pdf
Report source: Mega Bank (國際經濟金融週報)
Date: 2026-09-29

### Page 1

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
中華民國一一五年九月二十九日

國際經濟金融週報

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和訊號 
經濟 
⚫ S&P Global 公布，9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數(PMI Composite Output 
Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4，創逾 5 年新高；服務業及製造業
PMI 分別升至 58.7 及 57.0。在內需強勁帶動下，新訂單與就業同步成
長，惟供應鏈瓶頸及能源成本攀升推升通膨壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國新屋銷售年化率為 68.4 萬戶，月增 6.4%、年減 2.0%，顯示銷
售較前月回升但需求尚未完全恢復。中西部與南部為主要支撐，惟房價
年減、庫存仍維持 48 萬戶以上，供給相對充裕，後續銷售能否延續仍
待觀察。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.1，月減 7.0%、年減 12.7%，創近四個
月低點。當前狀況與預期指數同步下滑，反映高物價、燃料價格與貿易
不確定性持續壓抑信心；同時通膨預期升溫，後續消費動能仍待觀察。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 中國國家主席習近平於 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪美並與川普舉行峰會 ， 雙方
同意延長貿易休兵至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，推動部分商品降稅、建立 AI
對話機制及持續磋商稀土供應議題，並延續高層溝通。台灣議題未列入
成果清單，關稅、科技競爭及供應鏈等核心分歧仍未化解，後續談判發
展仍待觀察。 
短評 
⚫ 美國 9 月 PMI 強勁擴張，顯示企業活動與就業動能仍具韌性，但投入
成本與售價上升也反映通膨壓力升溫。相較之下，房市需求尚未全面恢
復，消費信心則明顯轉弱，顯示內需前景仍有隱憂；美中延長貿易休兵
雖有助穩定市場，但核心分歧未解，後續不確定性仍高。 
歐 
元 
區 
9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，服務業 PMI 增1.4 點至 53，製造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7。 
短評 
⚫ 儘管 9 月經濟表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲
央行的鷹派態度，使得 10 月升息一碼的可能性些微增加，亦強化未來
升息預期。

經濟 ⚫ 1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 習近平赴華府與美國總統川普舉行峰會，雙方同意貿易休戰期延長至
2027 年 1 月 10 日，並成立雙邊貿易理事會，但在科技管制、稀土供應
及關稅等核心議題上，仍未取得突破。 
⚫ 美中雙方就「300 億美元對 300 億美元」對等降稅架構達成共識，包
含中國製造的小型家電、玩具、節日裝飾品及兒童汽車安全座椅等，以
及美國生產的農產品、海鮮、原木、化妝品及醫療器材等。超過90%的
清單內產品將免除雙方此前相互加徵的關稅，並適用最惠國關稅待遇。 
短評 
⚫ 美中雙方貿易休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給
予中國稀土供應履約的壓力。 
⚫ 美中每年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程
設備、電動車、軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，本次降稅實質效益有限。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處

一、 美國—PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和
訊號 
標普全球 (S&P Global) 公布， 2026 年 9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數 (PMI 
Composite Output Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4， 連續第四個月攀升 ， 創2021
年 7 月以來新高；其中服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)由 56.5 升至 58.7，創
59 個月高點，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 由 53.9 升至 57.0，創
2022 年 5 月以來最高水準，顯示美國企業活動持續強勁擴張。受國內市場需求穩
健帶動，製造業與服務業新訂單同步加速成長，推升整體產出增速升至逾 5 年來
最快水準，企業亦積極擴增人力，就業成長速度創 2022 年 6 月以來新高。另一
方面，積壓訂單增加及供應鏈延遲加劇，反映產能利用率持續攀升；加以近期油
價上漲推升燃料及運輸成本，使企業投入成本增幅升至 2022 年 10 月以來最高，
帶動售價漲幅回升，顯示通膨壓力有所升溫。儘管如此，企業對未來一年營運前
景仍維持正向看法，反映市場對美國經濟韌性及需求持續擴張仍具信心。 
2026 年 8 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整年化率為 68.4 萬戶 ， 較上月增加6.4%，
但較去年同期下降 2.0%，顯示新屋銷售較前月回升，惟仍低於去年同期水準。地
區別表現分歧，其中中西部及南部分別較上月增加 84.9%及 6.9%，東北部及西部
則分別減少 36.1%及 15.2%。價格方面，8 月新屋銷售中位價為 39.37 萬美元，較
上月小升 0.4%，但較去年同期下降 5.8%。供給方面，8 月底經季節性調整後待售
新屋數量為 48.3 萬戶，與上月持平，較去年同期減少 2.0%；按目前銷售速度估
算，供給月數為 8.5 個月，低於上月的 9.0 個月，與去年同期大致持平。整體而
言，8 月美國新屋銷售較前月回升，主要受中西部與南部銷售增加帶動，惟仍低
於去年同期，顯示需求動能尚未完全恢復。價格方面，中位價較去年同期下滑，
反映市場價格仍有調整壓力；同時待售新屋維持在 48 萬戶以上，供給月數仍達
8.5 個月，顯示整體供給相對充裕，後續仍須觀察銷售回升是否具持續性。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 9 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.1，低於上月
的 51.7， 月減7.0%、 年減12.7%， 為近四個月低點 ， 並較2026 年 1 月下降約 15%，
顯示消費者信心持續轉弱。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由51.9 降至 50.9，月減

1.9%、 年減15.7%； 消費者預期指數則由51.5 降至 46.3， 月減10.1%、 年減10.4%，
反映民眾對未來景氣與個人財務前景的看法明顯惡化。調查指出，消費者對高物
價的擔憂持續升高，當前與未來一年個人財務看法均較上月轉弱約一成；短期企
業展望也因燃料價格偏高與貿易爭端再度升溫而明顯下滑。政治立場方面，各陣
營受訪者普遍認為年初以來經濟前景轉弱，其中共和黨人信心較 1 月下降 20%，
民主黨人下降 13%。 通膨預期方面 ， 未來一年通膨預期由8 月的 4.0%升至 4.6%，
為今年 6 月以來最高，且高於今年 2 月的 3.4%(美伊爆發衝突之前)及 2024 年各
月水準；長期通膨預期亦由 3.3%升至 3.4%，結束連續三個月持平，並高於 2024
年 2.8%至 3.2%的區間。整體而言，9 月美國消費信心再度降溫，顯示高物價、燃
料價格與貿易不確定性持續壓抑民眾信心，後續仍須觀察通膨預期升溫是否進一
步影響消費動能。 
中國國家主席習近平於 2026 年 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪問美國，並於 9 月 24 日
在華府白宮與美國總統川普舉行峰會。會後，美中雙方未發布聯合聲明，而是分
別公布成果清單，顯示雙方在穩定雙邊關係及維持高層溝通方面取得部分共識，
包括同意建構「基於尊重、公平、對等的建設性戰略穩定關係」、推動 300 億美
元非敏感商品對等降稅安排、建立人工智慧 (AI)或「超級智慧」對話與風險溝通
機制，以及持續就稀土與關鍵礦產供應鏈議題進行磋商。此外，美方並表示中國
將於 2027 年及 2028 年分別進口至少 1,000 萬公噸美國煤炭。值得注意的是，台
灣議題並未出現在雙方公布的成果文件中；另一方面，中方則額外強調兩軍危機
溝通機制及合作搜尋失蹤美軍遺骸等事項。整體而言，本次川習會有助延續美中
對話與降低短期緊張情勢，惟雙方在關稅、科技競爭、供應鏈安全及其他戰略議
題上的結構性分歧仍未完全化解，後續談判進展及雙邊互動方向仍有待持續觀察。 
二、歐元區—9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，且為連續四個月上
升。其中服務業PMI 增 1.4 點至 53，創近 10 個月高點，主要反映 AI 與國防投資
活動增加，帶動商業服務需求，抵銷能源價格上漲對消費購買力的負面影響。製
造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7，不過，德國萊茵河水位下降，沿岸較為發達的化工、製
藥業物流成本增加，因而暫時壓抑製造業上行空間。值得注意的是，由於國際原
油價格回升，綜合投入、產出物價指數皆上升至近 4 個月最高。故儘管 9 月經濟
表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲央行的鷹派態度，使得

三、日本—9 月綜合 PMI 維持擴張，製造業表現仍具韌性 
 日本 9 月綜合 PMI 由 53.5 降至 52.5，製造業與服務業同步回落，經濟成長動
能較前兩個月的高檔有所降溫。製造業 PMI 下跌 0.8 點至 54.1，雖然產出、新訂
單及出口訂單增速放緩，但外部需求仍相當強勁，尤其 AI 相關需求可能持續帶
動出口表現，加上企業開始重建庫存、供應鏈瓶頸尚未完全解除，預料補庫存需
求將在未來數月繼續支撐生產成長。服務業 PMI 下降 0.9 點至 51.6，受到工資、
運輸及能源成本上升影響 ， 需求動能略有降溫 ， 但積壓訂單與出口業務有所改善 。
另，投入成本與售價漲幅均較前期放緩，顯示通膨壓力正逐步緩解，但整體物價
水準仍維持高檔。此外，企業對未來景氣的信心持續改善，未來活動指數升至七
個月高點，反映企業普遍預期經濟擴張趨勢仍可望延續。 
四、中國—美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-7 月)略增 0.1 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 1.9 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 31.1%、71.8%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增20.1%、109.9%)、化學原料
及製品(營收、利潤年增 9.1%、51%)。此外，電氣機械及器材業、汽車業營收年
增率雖分別為 11.5%、2.9%，但獲利分別年減 5.2%、16%。非金屬礦物製品業營
收、獲利下滑 9.5、46.7%，黑色金屬加工業營收、獲利分別年減 1.1%、62.4%，
反映產業持續進行減產所致。整體而言，部分產業受惠於短期原物料價格波動，
以及受到政策推動的高科技產業表現較佳，惟部分產業因陷入產能調整週期，或
政府減少支持等因素而表現較弱，產業分化明顯。 
展望未來，儘管美中雙方互相降低等值 300 億美元產品關稅，惟考量雙方每
年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程設備、電動車、
軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，因此實質效益有限。另外，雖然美中雙方同意延長貿
易休戰，但休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給予中國稀土
供應履約的壓力。後續將關注美中能否在休戰期間就開放市場、科技競爭、供應

鏈安全等議題取得進一步共識，若談判不如預期，美國仍可能重新祭出新的反制

五、印度及東南亞—亞銀上修亞洲及太平洋經濟體 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.7% 
亞 洲 開 發 銀 行 (ADB) 最 新 經 濟 展 望 (Asian Development Outlook, ADO 
September 2026 )上修 2026 年亞洲及太平洋地區經濟體 2026 年經濟成長率(GDP)
至 4.7%(7 月預估 4.5%)、2027 年為 4.5%。ADB 認為，亞洲開發中經濟體雖面臨
中東衝突延長、俄烏戰爭再升溫、能源價格高檔震盪及強烈聖嬰現象等逆風，但
在政府刺激政策、民間投資及 AI 帶動科技出口支撐下，區域經濟仍展現韌性。
通膨方面，2026 年通膨率下修至 3.9%，惟能源與糧食價格壓力仍高，2027 年通
膨預測上修至 3.3%。 
區域經濟表現方面 ， 南亞成長最強勁 ，2026 年 GDP 成長率預估上修至 6.4%，
主要受印度內需與投資帶動；東亞則受中國內需疲弱影響，成長維持平穩；東協
國家(ASEAN)上調至 4.7%， 其中越南 、 馬來西亞及泰國受惠於科技與製造業投資
而獲上修。中亞及西亞、太平洋地區則面臨能源及食品價格上漲壓力，成長預測
略為下修至 3.7%、3.0%。(詳下表) 
ADB 認為未來最大風險包括地緣政治衝突升級、聖嬰 現象造成糧食供應衝
擊，以及 AI 相關資產可能出現價格重估。整體而言，AI 驅動的科技投資仍是亞
洲經濟體最重要的成長動能來源，而能源、糧食及氣候風險將成為影響各國通膨
與信用風險的重要變數。

資料來源: Asian Development Outlook( ADO), September 2026 
五、台灣—AI 浪潮推升接單動能，外銷訂單再創新高 
8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，首度突破千億美元大關，其中受惠於 ASIC
晶片需求帶動，資通產品接單翻倍、電子產品接單亦大增 83.9%，惟光學器材因
面板需求維持低檔，年減 5%。非科技產品方面，基本金屬年增 26.8%，係因銅箔
及銅箔基板受惠 AI 相關供應鏈需求及銅價續漲，加以鍍鋅鋼需求回溫；機械年
增 28.3%，係因半導體業者增加資本支出，帶動半導體設備、自動化設備需求延
續；化學品年增 15.4%，主因藥品接單成長，加上產業用化學品需求持續增加；
塑橡膠製品年增 6.7%， 主係塑膠原料受原料上漲而推升產品售價 ， 加上部分塑膠
製品訂單回溫。展望未來，經濟部統計處預估 2026 年外銷訂單有望突破 1 兆美
元，在 AI 相關需求持續擴張帶動下，科技產品表現可望維持強勁，而傳統產業
中下游產品亦可望受惠於相關供應鏈需求，惟上游原物料價格競爭激烈，後續仍
須關注第四季消費旺季是否有相關備貨需求。

國巨* (2327 TT) 
淡季不淡，第四季續強

永豐觀點 
AI 相關占比達 20%，較上季提升 4%。稼動率方面，將較上季 95%持續成長，拋
開過往明顯淡旺季，營運成長可期。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係看好國巨營運將受惠於(1) 日韓廠商將低階 MLCC 產能
移轉至高階產品的訂單外溢效應，(2) 預估 4Q26 稼動率逾 95%。另一方面，漲價
效應浮現將進一步推升毛利率表現， (3) AI 伺服器電源規格提升下，國巨在 AI 
Server 產品線齊全，除 MLCC 外，鉭質電容、電感及晶片電阻的用量增加。綜上
所述，國巨受惠轉單效應、被動元件產業循環向上、仍有持續漲價機會，故維持買
進的投資建議，以 2027-2028 年平均 EPS 預估，給予 27 倍本益比，目標價 940
元。

ESG 評析 
國巨企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。

國巨集團為台灣被動元件第一大供應商：3Q26 營收產品別占比 MLCC 21.5%，
Magnetics 22.3%，T antalum(Kemet) 23.7%，Resistor 15.3%，Sensor 10.6%，
Other Components 6.6%，其中 MLCC 與電阻成長主要來自標準品與特殊品需求
強勁 ； 終端應用占比 ：Industrial 28.7%，Computing & Enterprise System 28.3%，
Automotive 14.9%，Consumer 11.7%，T elecom 12.5%，Aerospace/Defense 
/Medical 3.9%，其中 AI 佔比約為 20%，較上季增加 4%。 
價格調漲及稼動率提升，3Q26 獲利優於預期：受惠於 AI 相關及工業 Industrial
需求強勁，帶動各產品線、應用市場營收成長，其中標準品及特殊品皆成長，AI 相
關營收比重達 19.8%。國巨 3Q26 營收 459.32 億元(+11.4% QoQ，+49.7% YoY)，
受惠產品組合及價格調整，毛利率 41%(+2.5pts QoQ，+4.8pts YoY)，優於預期
的 39.5%，營利率 29.5%(+3.1ptsQoQ，+6.6ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 119.8 億元
(+27.2%QoQ，+88.5%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 5.83 元，優於預期的 5.27 元。 
動能延續，4Q26 規模經濟效應顯現：展望第四季，國巨的 B/B Ratio 約為 2，仍
處於歷史高檔水準。稼動率方面，預將優於上季的 95%。在稼動率持續提升且先
前與客戶的漲價協議陸續發酵下 ， 研究部預估國巨4Q26 營收 532.44 億元(+7.5% 
QoQ，+48% YoY)，受惠產品組合及價格調整，毛利率 41.5%(+0.5pts QoQ ，
+4.2pts YoY)，營利 29.8%(+0.3ptsQoQ，+5.5ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 132.39 億元
(+10.5%QoQ，+96.1%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為 6.45 元 。 預估國巨2026 年營收 1853.98
億（+39.5%YoY） ，毛利率40%(+3.8pts YoY)，營利率28%(+5.6pts YoY)，稅後淨
利 426.4 億元(+80.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 20.76 元。 
產業循環向上空間可期：在 AI 伺服器電源規格不斷提升下，被動元件用量明顯增
加，日韓廠商皆發出漲價通知，國巨受惠於高階 MLCC 產品用量的增加外，鋁質
及鉭質電容等用量增加顯著。此外，預期在轉單效應顯現後，將使國巨在中低階產
品具有市場主導權。若以應用別區分，目前以 AI 相關終端需求最為強勁，國巨產
品亦將受惠鉭質電容 、 電感及晶片電阻的用量增加 ； 車用亦有復甦 。 展望2027 年，
國巨將於 2Q27 陸續開出新產能，預估總產能將年增 10-15%。此外，除 AI 相關
需求持續暢旺外，因競爭對手產能調整，產生的產能缺口難在短期補足，轉單效應
將至少延續至 2Q27，被動元件產業循環仍在持續向上，預期2027 年 MLCC 仍有
30%以上的漲價空間，其餘被動元件也有望因產能排擠使供給出現缺口，帶動下波
漲價 ， 並透過與客戶簽訂長期合約確保營運延續性 ， 故預估國巨2027 年營收 2422.2
億（+30.6%YoY），毛利率43.6%(+3.6pts YoY)，營利率 32%(+4pts YoY)，稅後

Valuation: We increase our 12m TP to NT$7,100 from NT$7,000 previously, following 
our upward earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x

Price Target Risks and Methodology - MediaTek (2454.TW) 
Valuation: Our 12m TP of NT$7,100 is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (1.8 stdv 
above its 5-year trading average) applied to our 2H27E-1H28E EPS. 
Key risks to our views: (1) Weaker-than-expected end demand especially with 
smartphones, (2) Higher foundry cost impacting its margin outlook, (3) Intensifying 
competition that would result in reduced profitability, and (4) Slower ramp in ASIC would 
result in changes in operating leverage

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 170: 261102_daiwa_avc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_daiwa_avc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on AVC. The company plans to release 
its 3Q26 results on 10 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our 
latest memo Record-high 3Q26 revenue beat expectations, 7 October). We 
expect AVC to maintain its leading position in the following years with more 
content value from ASICs projects since 4Q26.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. AVC’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD59,089m (+20.3% QoQ and +51.8% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 102.3% and 103.0%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD57,771m, +17.6% QoQ and +48.4% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus forecasts for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 33.5% (vs. 32.6% in 2Q26 and 26.1% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus forecast of 33.3%. We forecast its operating 
profit of TWD16.8bn (+25.0% QoQ and +108.3% YoY), 4.5% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus forecast of TWD16.1bn and we forecast its 
operating margin of 28.5% (vs. 27.4% in 2Q26 and 20.8% in 3Q25). 
Overall, we forecast its net profit of TWD11.6bn (+21.6% QoQ, +117.8% 
YoY), 4.3% higher than the consensus estimate of TWD11.2bn. For 4Q26, 
we forecast revenue to grow 15.1% QoQ (monthly revenue to rise MoM), 
and 1Q27 revenue to grow 2.9% QoQ considering ASIC and GPU projects.

Solid capacity expansion plan in 2027; worsening supply-demand 
environment to bring in a more favourable price trend to the sector. In 
light of the manifold content value from trays, we now expect content value 
by one of its ASICs clients to increase by another 20%. AVC expects the 
revenue drivers in 2027 to include double-sided cold plates, rising liquid 
cooling penetration from ASICs, and more content value wins (one-stop 
shopping model), for example, from CDU, CPU and NPO. As such, AVC 
plans to ramp up its cold plate capacity by 60% by 3Q27 with more 
automated product lines (positive to its further gross margin YoY 
expansion) to support its Vietnam plants, based on current order visibility 
and high utilisation rate. We keep our 2026-28E EPS unchanged.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD4,815 (from TWD4,465), still based on a target PER of 30x vs. a 
past-3-year PER range of 5-25x [average PER of 15x]) on its solid earnings 
visibility (earnings CAGR of 45.3% over 2026-2028E; 0.66x PEG) and 
applied to our 1-year forward EPS. Downside risks: slower-than-expected 
progress in high-gross-margin orders, slow AI server adoption, and 
competition.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above consensus, likely as we are 
relatively more aggressive in our revenue, gross and operating margins.

2 November 2026

3Q26 results preview: likely to beat expectations

➢ We expect AVC’s quarterly revenue to grow QoQ until end of 2027 
➢ More favourable price environment and margins profile in 2027 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD4,815

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

### Page 5

Asia Vital Components (3017 TT): 2 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 6 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of AVC are the same individual, but we see no issue with its board
quality. Besides, we see the Chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry, which is a
positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 2 AVC's dividend payout ratio was 40-52% over 2020-25, likely due to its capacity expansionplans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was 1-3% of total revenue during 2021-25,and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chainmanagement 1
AVC has introduced Hazardous Substance Process Management (HSPM) and received
QC080000 certification. In addition, AVC set up a Green Product team to promote HSPM and
formulate environmental regulation to make sure its material sourcing and efficiency are well-
performed.
E Data security 1
To prevent the interruption of information systems and leakage of sensitive information causing
losses to the company and customers, AVC has introduced an information security management
system and passed the ISO 27001 information security management system certification in
2019.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 183: 261103_daiwa_Realtek

Source PDF: all report pdf/261103_daiwa_Realtek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we fine-tune our forecasts for 
Realtek. We believe its near-term demand-rebalancing hiccups have come 
to an end, thanks to its pricing power and its ability to pass on supply-chain 
cost inflation. Given our interpretation of management’s tone that 4Q26 is 
likely to be sequentially flat, we expect Realtek’s business to return to its 
seasonal track from 1Q27. We expect it to continue capitalising on its 
structure growth driver of multi-year/multi-G (gigabits per second) 
connectivity bandwidth upgrade cycle, with any AI penetration adding to 
upside. We reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD798.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We fine-tune our 2026-28E EPS 
given that fundamentals are broadly intact heading into 2027. Recall 
management’s commentary at its results call (29 October) of expecting 
4Q26 to be a “prudent and steady” quarter. We interpret this as likely a 
sequentially flat revenue, counter-seasonal after its 3Q26 miss on deeper 
demand rebalancing. Without quantifying its 2027 business outlook, 
management expects the year to be one of steady growth. We forecast 
Realtek’s 2027 revenue to grow by 18% YoY, assuming its business returns 
to seasonal track after 2 years of demand rebalancing hiccups caused by 
customers’ unusual restocking and pre-stocking activities.

Back on track after recent hiccups. After 2 years of anti-seasonal 
performance on customer pre-stocking amid uncertainty over Trump’s 
reciprocal tariffs, followed by component price hikes and supply-chain 
uncertainty related to AI cannibalisation, we expect Realtek’s business to 
return to its seasonal track from 1Q27. We believe it should continue to 
capitalise on its secular driver of multi-year/multi-G bandwidth upgrade 
cycle from both wireline and wireless connectivity, including 2.5G/5G/10G 
Ethernet, 10GPON, managed switches and WiFi 7/8. Its penetration into AI, 
from cloud AI companion chips to edge AI to physical AI, should add to 
growth upside, although any meaningful contribution may take time, 
evidenced by its new product offering/development such as SSD, Ethernet 
hub, optical cable, i3C security and SerDes controllers, to name a few. 
According to management, AI will contribute a high single-digit % revenue 
this year, ramping to over 30% contribution by 2030.

What we recommend: Following forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for Realtek to TWD798 (previous: TWD750), on the same 4-quarter forward 
PER target of 20x. We reaffirm our Outperform (2) rating with key downside 
risk of any component price hikes leading to worse-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are broadly in line with consensus, 
ranging from -3% to +2%.

3 November 2026

Back on track after rebalancing

➢ Fine-tuning our forecasts on demand rebalancing after pre-stocking 
➢ Likely back on seasonal track after this year’s hiccups 
➢ Reaffirming Outperform (2) call with a higher 12-month TP of TWD798

Company profile 
Realtek focuses on research, design, development and distribution of integrated circuits (ICs) for 
applications across networking & communication (NC), computer & peripheral (CP) and multimedia & 
consumer (MM). The majority of its revenue comes from NC products, including WiFi, Bluetooth, 
Ethernet and switch controllers. It also provides diversified CP products such as audio codec, IP cam, 
and solid-state drive (SSD) controllers as well as MM products like TV SoC and monitor controllers.

### Page 5

5

Realtek Semiconductor (2379 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 29 Jan 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Realtek’s Board of Directors consists of nine members whose expertise spans industry,
academia, finance, accounting, and management, providing the company with a broad and
diverse range of professional perspectives. Three of these directors are independent,
representing 33 percent of the board and exceeding the regulatory minimum requirement of 20
percent for independent directors. This higher proportion of independent directors enhances
oversight, helps safeguard shareholder interests, and supports more objective decision‑making.
However, an important governance risk remains in the area of board diversity. The board
currently includes only one female director out of nine members, which represents 11 percent of
the total. This limited female representation may constrain the breadth of viewpoints and
experiences reflected in board discussions and could be perceived as misaligned with evolving
stakeholder expectations and best practices on gender diversity in corporate governance.
Capital management 1
In the past 8 years, Realtek's dividend payout ratios were above 80%. As a fabless company, it
does not need high capital expenditure for manufacturing sites. Realtek's business has shown
stable earnings growth over its 30-year history. Thus, we see its dividend policy with a high
payout ratio as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 2
Realtek’s related-party transactions are primarily with G.M.I. Technology Inc. and Greatek
Electronics Inc. Realtek pays certain processing costs to related parties. These payments are
made under standard commercial terms and conditions. Overall, we do not observe any
significant impact on Realtek’s business arising from these related-party transactions.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
Realtek has formulated a set of supplier management regulations in accordance with Realtek's
Supplier Sustainability Management Policy. It requires suppliers to obtain ISO9001 quality
management system certification to ensure that all of its suppliers possess a high level of
environment awareness.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
As a fabless company without manufacturing plants, Realtek does not produce much wastewater
compared to manufacturing companies, and thus has a relatively limited impact on the
environment. Realtek has implemented a water conservation plan to increase water efficiency,
such as the reuse of rainwater, re-circulating water in its cooling tower and installing water-
saving devices. Its water saving rate (=reduced water consumption/total water withdrawn)
increased gradually.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
The company reports to the Executive Yuan's Environmental Protection Administration waste
and reporting management system every month on the quantity of waste and its method of
disposal. In 2024, it produced 26.10 tonnes of industrial waste, maintaining over a 90% recycling
rate for hazardous industrial waste.
E GHG emissions 1
Realtek has independently conducted GHG emission inventory and management at its Hsinchu
Headquarters since 2019. In 2022, Realtek implemented a comprehensive ISO 14064-1:2018
GHG inventory and passed third-party verification. As of 2024, the company has completed
GHG emission inventory and third-party verification for all sites within the Realtek Group.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 184: 261103_daiwa_sunon

Source PDF: all report pdf/261103_daiwa_sunon.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide a 3Q26 results preview on Sunon, which plans to 
release its 3Q26 results on 6 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call as we 
expect the 4Q26 and 2027 outlook for its telecom, servers, industrial and 
auto segments to remain intact with both gross and operating margin set to 
expand. We now forecast a net profit CAGR of 28.2% over 2026-28E from 
21.2% previously (see our flash, 28 August).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. Sunon’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD6,143m (+13.4% QoQ and +24.5% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 97.2% and 102.2%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD6,319m, +16.6% QoQ and +28.1% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus estimates for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 32.8% (vs. 31.3% in 2Q26 and 31.3% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of 31.9%. We forecast an operating 
profit of TWD1,115m (+33.3% QoQ and +45.0% YoY), 3.0% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD1,082m and an operating 
margin of 18.1% (vs. 15.4% in 2Q26 and 15.6% in 3Q25). Overall, we 
forecast a net profit of TWD901m (+30.5% QoQ, +29.7% YoY), 1.9% 
higher than the consensus estimate of TWD884m. We forecast 4Q26 
revenue to grow 7.5% QoQ, and 1Q27 revenue to grow 1.4% QoQ 
considering its ASIC and GPU projects.

Rising liquid cooling revenue and EC fan exposure in 2027E underpin 
its gross margin expansion. We expect Sunon’s cold plate products (ie, 
enterprise and GPU clients), pump and CDU to be the key revenue drivers 
with certification also underway for CSP clients. We expect revenue from 
EC fans to reach 10% in 2027E for the industrial segment. We revise up 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-13%, mainly on higher revenue, gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD265 (from TWD236), still based on a target PER of 18x vs. its past-
5-year PER range of 6-25x, on the 2027E EPS, given solid earnings 
visibility from intact demand in the server, telecom, industrial and auto 
segments. We believe its dividend yield of 5.6-9.2% over 2026-28E will 
offer downside support for its share price. Downside risks: worse-than-
expected gross-margin expansion and AI server development progress.

How we differ: We are one of only two overseas brokers covering the 
stock. Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-12% above the Bloomberg consensus, 
likely due to our more aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating 
margin estimates.

Company profile 
Founded in 1980 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2000, Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry 
(Sunon) is one of the top-4 fan and motor providers in the world, along with Delta Electronics, Nidec and 
ebm-papst. Its major clients are Apple, Dell, Lenovo, Bosch, Siemens and Tesla. Its applications include 
IT & OE, telecoms & servers, industrial & medical equipment, home applications, distribution, autos and 
LED lighting.

### Page 5

5

Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry (2421 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 8 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Sunonwealth are the same individual; Sunonwealth's board is led
by the Hung family. We see no issues with the company’s board. We view the chairman leading
the R&D team as a sign of the company’s continuing focus on developing new products to
achieve a long-term competitive advantage.
Capital management 2 Sunonwealth's dividend payout ratio was 83-88% in 2015-18, but it decreased to 60-69% during2022-25 due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during2023-25.
E Materials sourcing &efficiency 1
Sunonwealth’s sourcing of raw materials has been relatively stable (including IC, copper, plastic,
aluminium and steel), and we also note that Sunonwealth abides by RoHS regulations and
relevant international standards (ISO14001, IECQ QC080000, ISO9001) for its material sourcing
activities. With the ongoing ESG trend, its clients may move towards a more energy-efficient
production cycle, which will likely benefit Sunonwealth’s energy-efficient products.
E Product design &lifecycle management 2
Sunonwealth has improved its product designs to stronger but lighter form factors. Besides, we
have observed more green projects utilising Sunonwealth's capabilities. Thus, we believe
Sunonwealth has strong product design and lifecycle management capabilities to address the
ongoing changing business environment.
S Product quality &safety 2 Sunonwealth has its own Quality Strategy Centre to evaluate quality issues. With internationalstandards in place, we believe Sunonwealth has taken relevant measures to address productquality and safety issues.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

M

Winbond Electronics Corp  | Asia Pacific
Strong 3Q margins; growth to
moderate in 4Q
3Q26 earnings beat: 3Q revenue was NT$82bn, +38% Q/Q, +278% Y/Y, 8% below
MSe; GM was 70.6%, +4.3ppt Q/Q, +23.9ppt Y/Y, 12.5ppt above MSe; EPS was NT
$7.49, +39% Q/Q, +1045% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. CMS revenue grew 50% Q/Q as ASP
increased by ~60%, despite lower bit shipments, which was due to reserving supply
for L TA customers. Flash revenue rose 34% Q/Q on pricing and mix with flat bit

shipments. Memory GM reached 73.9%.
4Q growth to moderate; 16nm supports 2027: Management expects continued 4Q
growth, but a slower pace as non-AI demand softens. DRAM contract prices should
rise more moderately than in 3Q, while high density NOR remains tight. KH module
A capacity should reach 24-25kwpm by end-2026. 16nm ramp from ~2kwpm to
16kwpm is expected to support 2H27 shipments.
AI opportunities broaden; module  B capex approved: Management projects server

NOR bit shipments to grow 67% Y/Y in 2027. CUBE customers are increasingly
inference accelerator developers with several expected to begin small volume
production in 2027. Silicon capacitor capacity is largely allocated, but current
margins remain below conventional DRAM. The board newly approved NT$73bn
capex for the previously announced Module B expansion.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Winbond Electronics Corp (2344.TW)
Base case, 2.9x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with the approach we adopt for Greater China
memory IDM peers, in view of the industry's high volatility. We believe the stock merits a mul-
tiple beyond the high end of its historical band of 0.5-1.5x since 2017, as we are seeing DRAM
pricing upside, a sustainable NOR price hike, and opportunities in SLC NAND and SiCap
foundry business.

Risks to Upside
n NOR Flash pricing increase, driven by stronger-than-expected demand
n Stronger-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing supply shortage
n Faster-than-expected SLC NAND development
Risks to Downside
n NOR Flash downcycle, driven by weaker demand
n Lower-than-expected DRAM pricing given ongoing oversupply
n Slower-than-expected SLC NAND development

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Chuanchuan.Chen@yuanta.com 
陳幸宏 
OnielChen@yuanta.com

3Q26 EPS 16.9 元，優於預期 3 成 
3Q26 EPS  16.95 元，優於市場預期約 3 成，最大差異在於營收季增 35%，
優於 teens 季增之目標，主要動能來自 CPU 需求強勁、新增 Inference 專案
量產及獲得台積產能支持。本季毛利率季減 1.0 個百分點達 20.6%，主要受
到產品組合因素。以 3Q26 主要製程別變化，包括 N3 佔比由上季 51%升至
55%、N5 由 5%升至 12%，主要反映 CSP CPU 拉貨動能強勁。 
4Q26 CPU 續強、non CPU 新量產，營收達全年高峰 
展望 4Q26 為傳統入帳高峰及延續 CPU 需求強勁、MSFT Maia 及中國車用
專案放量，營收為全年高峰，根據先前假設全年 NRE 年增近持平、 4Q26 
Turnkey 佔比近 9 成，推估 4Q26 營收季增 1~2 成及 26 全年營收年增近 1
倍。毛利率受到產品結構因素，估計持平至略降、費用持平至略增，推估
EPS 18.61 元/季增 9.8%。 
COT 分工模式、公司 APT 競爭優勢，中長期展望維持樂觀 
2026 年營收成長來自 CSP N3 CPU ，估計營收佔比>5 成。AI 擴大至推論、
Agentic 應用及 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向 COT 分工模式，加上產能獲得支
援，使得 公司 27~28 年展望較先前樂觀，主要成長來自 CPU 需求強勁且含
蓋至少 2~3 家 CPU 客戶。2H27 美系車用客戶 AI 專案放量、MSFT Maia 及
其他 inference 專案為潛在動能，而 Google TPU 、Meta 等潛在效益落在 29
年 。 元 大 最 新 預 估 2026/27/28 EPS 59.5/142.1 /208.4 元 ， 分 別 上 修
9%/26%/28%，係因上修 27/28 年 CPU 出貨量及 Maia 營收貢獻，除了
CPU 佔比>4 成，Maia 二年佔比約 18%/22%，毛利率降 1 個百分點、費用
假設略降。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS  16.95 元，優於預期約 3 成。預期 4Q26 CPU 需求強勁、
新增 inference 專案、MSFT Maia 系列及中國車用專案放量，營運展
望樂觀，估計 2026 全年營收 YoY 接近倍數成長。  
⧫  
隨著 AI 擴大至推論/代理型應用，帶動 CPU 需求爆發及營運上修，延
續至 27~28 年接續美系車用專案量產、MSFT，29 年來自 CSP 轉向
COT 效益推升營運達另一高峰。 
⧫  
預估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 59/142/208 元，上修 9%/26%/28%，維持買
進，目標本益比降至 45 倍、2028 年 EPS，推得目標價 9360 元。近
期 AI 族群受系統風險、籌碼面因素而使評價面轉趨保守。然看好中長
期 Agentic AI、COT 趨勢明確，帶動公司營運更具爆發力。

個百分點達 20.6%，主要受到產品組合因素。以 3Q26 主要製程別變化，包括 N3 佔比由上季 51%

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 2 日 Global Unichip 第3 頁，共 14頁  
Agentic AI  帶動 CPU 價值重估、公司營運上修 
隨 AI 大型模型成熟漸漸由訓練轉向 Inference/agentic AI ，預期全球 Token 需求每三個月翻倍，其
中 ASIC 可針對客戶需求打造客製化晶片，算力使用效率較 GPU 更貼近客戶需求、較佳的性價比。
全球 AI ASIC 市場 25~28 年 CAGR 達 35~40%，估計 ASIC 滲透率將突 4 成。Agentic AI 在於主
動任務執行，相較於過往 LLM 矩陣運算，強調 CPU/GPU/ASIC 的協同運算需求，帶動 CPU 搭配
比例提升、創意營運上修空間。 
CSP 朝 COT 模式，帶動更多 IC 設計服務委外的商機 
CSP 由傳統晶片廠統包朝向 COT(Customer Owned Tooling) 分工模式，轉由 CSP 主導晶片架構與
前端設計流程，將資源集中於最具價值的 Architecture/RTL 前端設計，將實體設計、先進封裝、
chiplet 後端設計服務分拆外包，基於晶片複雜度提升及開發成本墊高，藉由分工達分散供應鏈風險，
對傳統 ASIC 廠商造成價值鏈瓜分，對 backend 委外需求增加反而有利於純設計服務廠商。創意布
局包括 HBM2/3/3E/4/4E、GLink 相關 IP、CoWoS/SOIC/DoD 解決方案，自有 IP 具客製化彈性，
在 TSMC APT 生態系的重要提升，承接底層設計機會大增，為 COT 模式下的主要受惠者之一。 
COT 分工模式、公司 APT 競爭優勢，中長期展望維持樂觀 
2026 年營收成長來自 CSP N3 CPU ，估計營收佔比>5 成。AI 擴大至推論、Agentic 應用及 CSP
由傳統晶片統包轉向 COT 分工模式，加上產能獲得支援，使得 公司 27~28 年展望較先前樂觀，主
要成長來自 CPU 需求強勁且含蓋至少 2~3 家 CPU 客戶。2H27 美系車用客戶 AI 專案放量、MSFT 
Maia 及其他 inference 專案為潛在動能，而 Google TPU 、Meta 等潛在效益落在 29 年。元大最新
預估 2026/27/28 EPS 59.5/142.1/208.4 元，分別上修 9%/26%/28%，係因上修 27/28 年 CPU 出
貨量及 Maia 營收貢獻，除了 CPU 佔比>4 成，Maia 二年佔比約 18%/22%，毛利率降 1 個百分點、
費用假設略降。

Agentic AI 將帶動 CPU 需求大幅提升 
Agentic AI 不再只是單次 prompt-response，需要反覆進行任務規劃、推理、檢查與修正。相較傳
統生成式 AI，工作流程更長、步驟更多，對系統層級調度能力要求大幅提升。 CPU 在 Agentic AI 
中主要負責 orchestration、資料前後處理，以及外部工具與資料源調用。包含  Web、Database、
API 等任務管理，協助 GPU / ASIC 專注於模型運算。隨 agent 工作流變長，CPU 與 GPU 的協同
效率將影響整體延遲、吞吐量，成為未來 AI server 架構升級重點。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 2 日 Global Unichip 第4 頁，共 14頁  
圖 5：Agentic AI 的執行流程需要反覆的調用 CPU 與 GPU 的運算資源

資料來源：Semianalysis、元大投顧

解決推論延遲需提高 CPU 用量並優化演算法 
代理式 AI 涉及大量任務分解、環境切換、API 互動與外部工具調用，相關 orchestration latency 可
占總延遲約 50%-90%。搜尋網頁、分析內容、執行  Python 腳本與查詢資料庫等非模型推論任務，
多由 CPU 負責執行，因此增加 CPU 資源可直接緩解瓶頸。透過 COMB (CPU 感知微批次處理) 與 
MAS 可優化 CPU-GPU 協同演算法：COMB 將大批次任務拆分，使 CPU 與 GPU 可更同步執行，
研究顯示可提升約 1.8 倍運算效率。MAS 則依任務屬性區分 CPU-heavy 與 GPU-heavy workload，
建立彈性資源調度，避免任務壟斷整體運算資源。 
圖 6：當模型執行更複雜任務需要更多 CPU

資料來源：Semianalysis、元大投顧

不同的代理 AI 場景，CPU:GPU 之比例約落在 0.8-1.4:1 
代理式 AI 的 CPU 需求取決於工作流中工具調用與資料處理比例。以網頁資料蒐集為例， DOM 
parsing、摘要生成、網路 I/O 與資料整理多由 CPU 負責，CPU 延遲占比較高。因此若應用場景偏
向資料蒐集、網頁解析與外部工具互動， CPU:GPU 最適比例將提高至約  1.4:1，以降低整體推論
瓶頸。 
Excel 自動化、 Python / Bash 腳本執行與自動生成簡報，同樣需要大量  CPU 參與。相關任務
CPU 延遲占比約 25%-65%。因此，自動化與文件生成的 agent workflow，也可將 CPU:GPU 比例
提高至約 0.8:1，以改善整體任務效率。

產業概況  
IC 設計服務商透過新接案及轉量產來創造獲利，中長期受惠 ASIC 委外開發趨勢 
隨著半導體產業對晶片功能和性能要求增加，晶片結構日漸複雜、造價亦越來越昂貴，使得 IC 設
計工作困難度日益提高，進而成為 IC 設計服務業者重要之商機與切入點。IC 設計服務商可從最初
的產品概念、規格制定、開發、驗證、製造、生產到最終成品等各階段中協助下游客戶開發晶片，
客戶可彈性地選擇在任何環節切入半導體設計的產業鏈，並由設計服務業者完成後續統包作業。  
IC 設計服務商之營運以替客戶代為設計 IC 為主，其本身並沒有產品，整體業務通常可分為委託設
計(NRE，Non-Recurring Engineering) 及量產服務(Turnkey)，NRE 主要透過提供設計廠各種矽智
財(IP)及設計元件資料庫以接案開發產品，並收取一次性 NRE 費用來營利；而透過 NRE 委外設計
的 IC 晶片，經過 1-2 年設計開發完成後，若客戶本身並不具備與晶圓代工、封測等廠商業務往來
的經驗，但又想爭取有競爭力的投片價格與優異的技術服務，便會進一步透過和 IC 設計服務商代
為投片量產，以達成大量生產之需求，這部分業務則稱為 Turnkey。 
一般而言，NRE、Turnkey 之毛利率分別約落在 30%-40%、10%-20%，然根據不同案件、客戶、
規模、製程、應用、提供服務之數量等，仍會使利潤略有所差異。此外，儘管 Turnkey 毛利率較低，
但由於案件已進入到量產階段，IC 設計服務商僅需耗費少部分營運量能便可帶來可觀的營收貢獻，
故 NRE 案件最終是否能順利轉 Turnkey 亦將成為廠商在接案上的重要考量因素。 
隨 5G/AI/HPC 需求於近年激增及晶圓代工廠持續往更先進製程邁進，未來將會有越來越多的系統 /
品牌廠出現差異化晶片或自行研發晶片的需求，研調機構 Global Information 指出，全球 ASIC 晶
片市場將於 2030 年達到 355 億美元，2023~2030 年 CAGR 可望達 8.2%，中長期而言 IC 設計服
務產業有望受惠半導體產業大勢。

圖 8：設計服務商負責設計後之統包服務 圖 9：營運模式以 NRE 及 Turnkey 為主

獲利調整與股票評價  
維持買進，2028 年目標本益比降至 45 倍，推得目標價 9360 元 
3Q26 EPS  16.9 元，優於預期，主要差異在於 CPU 出貨優於預期及新增 inference 訂單，使得營
收季增 35%，優於原先預估 teens 成長，毛利率落在 20.6%則符合預期。展望 4Q26 延續 CPU 動
能更為強勁，加上台積產能支持，預估 2026 全年營收 YoY 有機會成長近 1 倍。中長期產業趨勢包
括 AI 應用擴大、ASIC 滲透率提升及委外趨勢及先進封裝重要性提升，CSP 策略由傳統晶片廠由
統包轉向 COT 分包方式，公司策略則以台積電先進封裝及後段實體設計為競爭優勢，承接底層設
計，提供更好的成本結構，可望推升營運達另一高峰。2026~27 年主要成長動能來自 CPU，隨 AI
擴大至推論或代理 AI，CPU 用量持續上修，北美車用 AI 專案預計 2Q27 量產，進入新一輪成長週
期，另外，MSFT Maia 、新增 inference 專案為 27~28 年潛在動能，COT 模式對公司 29 年訂單展
望 更 加 樂 觀 ， 包 括 Google TPU 、 Mata 等 。 元 大 最 新 預 估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 分 別 為
59.5/142.1/208.4 元，其中 follow 26/27/28 年 TPU 預估量分別>4M/>7M/>8Ｍ，CPU/TPU 搭配比
例提升至 1:2，相當於 CPU 出貨量較先前預估增加接近 1 倍，此外，公司 CPU 除了 Google，還
包括 MSFT 等 2~3 家、供應鏈產能獲得舒解，因而調高 CPU 預估，另外，調高來自 MSFT Maia
系列營收貢獻預估，估計 27/28 年分別為 18%/22%，毛利率估計降 1 個百分點，主要 CPU 佔比略
升。目前以 2026/27/28 年 EPS 換算，本益比分別為 129/54/37 倍。以 28 年評價基礎落在近一年
本益比區間 30-50 倍中下緣，約與國內同業平均本益比約 40.9 倍相當、國外同業平均本益比的
30.5 倍。隨著 CPU 規模上修、non CPU 包括 MSFT Maia 、車用等進入量產，公司營運進入更強
勁、更長遠的成長週期。維持買進，惟目標本益比由 50 倍降至 45 倍，主要反映近期半導體/AI 族
群受系統風險、籌碼面因素，使投資氛圍轉趨保守、評價面有所下修，以 28 年 EPS 評價基礎，推
得目標價 9360 元，然 AI ASIC 專案轉量產推升公司 2027~28 年營運成長趨勢明確，反映 Agentic、
COT 趨勢，有利公司未來發展並且帶動營運進入更強勁成長週期，基本面看法不變。

公司簡介  
創意電子(GUC)是先進 ASIC 領導廠商，主要提供全面 ASIC 設計服務，包括 Spec-in 和系統單晶片整合(SoC Integration) 、
實體設計(Physical Design)、先進封裝技術(Advanced Packaging Technology) 、量產服務以及 HBM 和 die-to-die 互連 IP。
客戶涵蓋人工智慧、高速運算、5G /網路、固態硬碟和工業領域等市場，據點遍及北美、中國、歐洲、韓國和日本等地。

### Page 11

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 創意整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在領先的位置，亦於
半導體設計與製造行業的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 創意的整體曝險較低，遠優於半導體設計與製造行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含營運方面的人力資本管理、資源使用以及商業道德等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：創意在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。主因公司的揭露程度佳，表
明對投資者及公眾的強烈責任感，同時公司於 ESG 亦有相關舉措(節電、綠色生產降低產品功耗)，但尚不能藉此彌平所
有風險。儘管如此，創意並未涉及任何與 ESG 相關的重大爭議。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
6515 穎崴(WinWay)

報告日期： 2026/11/03 研 究 員：翁浩軒  (Davy Wong)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 AI 晶片帶動高階測試需求暢旺。 
 產品組合轉佳加上探針自製比重提升有助毛利率回升。 
 2026 下半年及 2027 上半年新增產能，帶動今明年營收創高。

一. 3Q26 毛利率有望回升，EPS 27.63 元 
穎崴 3Q26 營收 45.56 億(QoQ+29.32%、YoY+152.58%)，營收再創
單季歷史新高 ， 受惠於 AI 晶片對 Socket 需求暢旺 ， 加上 Probe Card
拉貨挹助，預期毛利率在 Probe Card 比重降低下回升至 39.7%，費
用率降至 13%，稅後淨利 10.07 億(QoQ+49.8%、YoY+170.86%)，
EPS 27.63 元。

二、4Q26 營收季持平，預估 2026 年 EPS 95.86 元 
展望 4Q26，儘管 Socket 需求持續放量，但在營收基期已高，加上
Probe Card 案拉貨放緩下，預估營收將季持平，而毛利率在產品組
合優化加上探針自製比重提升下預估將升至 41.3% 。 預估穎崴 4Q26
營收 46.2 億(QoQ+1.4%、YoY+106.84%)，毛利率 41.3%，稅後淨利
11.08 億(QoQ+10.01%、YoY+129.20%)，EPS 30.4 元。預估 2026 年
營收 156.8 億(YoY+99.57%)，毛利率 40.49%，稅後淨利 34.86 億
(YoY+108.36%)，EPS 95.86 元。

三.  2027 年新產能挹助獲利創高，預估 EPS 187.56 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 對高階測試需求仍強勁，晶片製程複雜性增加，
將帶動 Probe Card、FT/SLT/Burn-in Socket 及 Thermal 解決方案需
求，穎崴為多家 AI 晶片廠供應商，營收獲利可望持續創高。客戶
別 而 言，美 系 GPU、CPU、TPU 對 Socket 需 求 持 續，CPU Probe Card
將持續貢獻穩。另外公司切入 CPO 後段測試，目前於客戶端驗證
中，隨著未來訊號將透過光做高速傳輸，穎崴 CPO 測試解決方案
有望成為中長線新的成長動能。公司 2027 年 Socket 及探針產能預
估較 2026 年初翻倍，且隨自製探針比重提升，有助毛利率改善。
預估穎崴 2027 年營收 266.23 億(YoY+69.78%)，毛利率 42.45%，稅
後淨利 68.36 億(YoY+96.12%)，EPS 187.56 元。

研發多年，為高效能 IC 測試治具的半導體測試介面設備商，公司
在高頻高速同軸測試座市場具領先地位，且與多家全球 IC 設計領
導廠商合作密切，在客戶研發階段即參與測試解決方案之開發。
隨著 AI 需求帶動半導體先進封裝需求放量，高階晶片所需的測試
種類、測試時間、複雜度均提升，均使穎崴測試治具及探針卡出
貨成長。考量到 1)晶片測試需求長期成長、2)公司毛利率可望落
底回升、3)2027 年新產能挹助帶動公司獲利創高，目前評價偏低，
參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 32 倍本益比，目標價 6000 元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
穎崴成立於 2001 年 4 月，總部位於高雄楠梓加工出口區，主要從
事半導體測試治具(Test Socket)及探針卡(Probe Card)之設計、開發
及生產製造關鍵技術，從晶圓測試(CP)、最終測試(FT)、系統測試
(SLT)、老化測試(Burn-In)及溫控系統(Thermal Control System)，提
供各種高速、高頻、廣溫域的測試介面；主要客戶為全球高階 IC
設計公司及封測大廠，為提供高效能 IC 測試治具的專業半導體測
試介面製造商。公司產品包括半導體測試介面、精密彈簧針、探
針卡、溫控模組，2026 上半年之營收比重分別為：測試治具 64%、
探針卡 27%、接觸元件 6%、其他 3%。公司為全球第一大邏輯測
試座供應商，主要競爭對手為日韓歐美廠商，國內同業包括精測、
旺矽、雍智科技等。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 帶動半導體測試需求 
受惠於 AI / HPC 需求爆發 2.5D/3D 先進封裝、Chiplet 架構應用快
速導入市場，晶片複雜度提升且封裝面績增加下，晶片尺寸放大
且測試接腳數亦快速增加 ， 另外晶片大廠在 AI GPU 、 CPU 、 與 ASIC
迭代速度加快，均使晶片測試規格提升且測試時間增加，帶動最
終測試（FT）、系統級測試（SLT）與老化測試（Burn-in）耗材需
求需求長期成長。穎崴深耕半導體測試介面、探針卡、精密彈簧
針及溫控模組技術多年，為高效能 IC 測試治具的半導體測試介面
領導廠商。除了後段測試，因應先進封裝成本昂貴，若在封裝後
才發現晶片缺陷將面臨巨大報廢損失，因此半導體廠加強在晶圓
切割前即進行晶片測試， 確認為 KGD ， 穎崴近年亦跨入 Probe Card
領域，與探針卡大廠 Technoprobe 簽訂 MEMS 探針卡供貨合約，
亦使探針卡營收比重近兩年提升至 20~30%水準。

2. 半導體測試介面市場高速成長  
公司的產品提供測試時所需要的硬體介面，稱為測試治具(Test 
Socket)，是 Device 與測試板之間的一個介面，結構中含有重要的
接觸電性元件(Contact Element)：Spring Probe，Spring Probe 提供
Device 和測試板(Test Board)間的訊號傳遞 ， 用於 FT/SLT 之 Socket ，
隨著 AI 晶片使用更多的 SLT 測試，將帶動公司的 Hyper Socket、
Coaxial Socket 、 Thermal System 需求 。根據研調機構預估 ，SLT&SFT 
2026~2030 CAGR 高於 15%。

3. 穎崴走在技術前端，與客戶共同未來需求產品  
穎崴與多家全球 IC 設計領導廠商合作密切，在客戶研發階段即參
與測試解決方案之開發，直接受惠於先進封裝、矽光共同封裝等
趨勢。公司之同軸測試座（Coaxial Socket）為目前 AI/HPC 晶片的

主流測試介面，支援最高 224Gbps 數據傳輸速率及高熱功耗處理
能力; 新一代超導測試座 (HyperSocket)提供晶片更柔和的接觸介
面，能顯著提高測試穩定性，同時實現阻抗精準匹配，有效降低
串擾，並大幅提升耐電流能力，亦可延長探針使用壽命並降低更
換成本 ， 將應用於未來大尺寸封裝與高 Pin 數晶片測試; 另外公司
亦提供多樣化測試座產品線，包括可耐受廣溫域及高功率的老化
測試座（Burn-in Socket） 與 PoP &塑膠測試座 。 共同封裝光學（CPO）
方面，公司全面布局 CPO 測試流程，主要著墨於 Die Level 測試，
推出雙面測試系統與光電測試座，在 M o d u l e  L e v e l  則以  
HyperSocket 切入。

4. 高雄楠梓新廠自製針產能持續開出  
隨著半導體需求暢旺，晶片測試複雜度提升，高階測試產品拉貨
強勁，公司積極擴大產能，新增產能主要來自仁武租賃廠房及楠
梓廠區，仁武二廠已動土，總面積 6300 坪(為一廠的 6 倍)，預估
2Q27 開始量產 。 今明年新產能包括探針及 Test Socket ， 探針部分，
探針 1H26 600 萬針/月，2H26 達 900 萬針/月，1H27 將達 1400 萬針
/月，除了滿足客戶需求，規模放大有助平均成本下降，自製比重
增加亦有助毛利率提升，Socket 產能方面，1H26 產能 3.3K 組/月，
2H26 達 4.2K 組/月，1H27 達 7.2K 組/月。公司未來仍將依市場需
求持續擴產，預估至 2030 年產能逐年提升，公司在後段測試元件
產能將維持領導地位。

## Document 292: AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates
Date:    1 day ago
AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
AI Jitters Continue While Fed
Pauses Hiking Rates
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 29 Oct 2026
View in browser

I. The AI Boom Versus The AI Trade
AI jitters will be back tomorrow after taking a breather so far this week, as
encouraging developments surrounding the AI investment boom helped
restore confidence and lift technology stocks. The S&P 500 rose 0.3% on
Wednesday, moving back above its 50-day moving average (chart).

### Page 2

The Nasdaq-100 advanced 0.6%, recovering toward its 50-day moving
average while remaining above its 200-day moving average (chart).
Among chipmakers, Micron jumped 5.9%, AMD gained 3.2%, and
Broadcom rose 2.8% (chart). SK Hynix shares surged as much as 9.4% in
Seoul following blockbuster earnings.
Three recent developments helped revive confidence in the AI trade:
(1) More spending. $235 billion in prospective AI financing emerged this
week, including Anthropic's expected $100 billion IPO raise, Meta's plans

to raise up to $95 billion through equity issuance, and SpaceX's $40 billion
debt deal to purchase GPUs. These developments suggest capital
remains available for the AI buildout, with equity markets providing an
alternative if debt financing tightens.

### Page 3

(2) Encouraging AI economics. Anthropic's S-1 filing offered five
encouraging signals about the sustainability of compute demand. First, H1
revenues surged from $1.2 billion to $16.3 billion y/y. Second, operating
losses narrowed from $2.1 billion to $693 million, with Q2 marking its first
quarterly operating profit. Third, 11,900 customers now spend at least
$100,000 annually, up from roughly 950 a year earlier. Fourth, revenue
per dollar of compute expense increased more than fivefold to $2.08.

Fifth, Anthropic held $131.7 billion in cash and short-term investments to
finance expansion.
(3) Strong semiconductor earnings. SK Hynix's Q3 results reinforced
confidence in AI infrastructure demand. Operating profit surged more than
sevenfold y/y, while the company announced roughly $75 billion in
shareholder returns. Most encouragingly, management expects memory
shortages through 2030, with production effectively sold out through 2029,
suggesting durable demand and pricing power.

These developments reinforce our view that the AI investment boom is
here to stay. But that doesn't mean that the AI trade won't remain jittery.
This evening, Alphabet shares are down about 10% after the company
missed Q3 consensus revenues and earnings estimates. Free cash flow
was also more negative than expected, while the company remains
committed to significant capex of over $200 billion this year and $300
billion in 2027. Long-term debt rose to $125.1 billion, up from $21.6 billion

a year earlier.
There was some good news. Cloud revenue more than doubled to $30.7
billion. Google said its Gemini app reached 1 billion users, making it the
fastest-growing product in its history. Furthermore, Microsoft shares rose
4% in extended trading this evening after the company reported better-
than-expected quarterly results, driven by 48% growth in its Azure cloud
infrastructure business.
II. The Fed Pauses Before Midterm Elections
The FOMC voted 10-2 Wednesday to hold the federal funds rate at

3.75%-4.00%, with Lisa Cook and Beth Hammack favoring a 25bps hike.
Chair Kevin Warsh acknowledged robust economic growth, a healthy
labor market, stubborn inflation, and financial conditions that aren't tight.
Yet he cited tightening financial conditions, anchored inflation

### Page 4

expectations, and improving productivity as reasons for patience. Here's
more:
(1) Economic activity. Warsh observed that "economic growth has
strengthened." Consumer spending remains robust and business
investment is growing at a double-digit pace. The capex story, he said, is
"not just robust, not just strong, but it is broadening."
(2) Labor market. Unemployment is lower than a year ago, payroll growth
has improved, and initial jobless claims remain near historic lows.

Employment conditions are "quite positive," Warsh said, with FOMC
discussions focused primarily on price stability.
(3) Inflation. "Inflation is too high," Warsh acknowledged, warning that
rising energy prices could spread through the economy. He rejected a
higher implicit inflation target: "There's no soft inflation target, there's no
implicit inflation target." Still, he believes inflation expectations remain
anchored.
(4) Financial conditions. Warsh noted that tighter financial conditions are

prompting more selective capital allocation. Rejecting claims that AI
investment is insensitive to interest rates, he observed that "interest rates
get in the cracks" as credit spreads have widened for some borrowers.
Nevertheless, "while overall financial conditions are tighter, I'm unwilling
and unable to call them tight."
(5) Productivity. Warsh disputed that robust growth necessarily implies
overheating: "That isn't the judgment of most of my colleagues." Improving

productivity, much of it predating recent AI advances, may have raised
potential growth without fueling inflation.
(6) Revamping the Fed. Warsh reiterated his opposition to forward
guidance outside crises. Yet other officials' comments shifted market
expectations away from an October hike despite hotter-than-expected
September CPI and PPI inflation reports, suggesting forward guidance
remains intact. He also plans to dedicate one FOMC meeting in 2027 and

two in 2028 to monetary policy strategy.
In our view, stronger productivity growth will let the economy expand
faster without adding inflationary pressure. That could be an argument

### Page 5

against restrictive monetary policy, but not against returning to neutral,
which we estimate at 4.25%-4.50%. We agree that September's hike
“removed a dose of accommodation,” but believe another 50bps is
needed to remove the rest. The Fed doesn't need to throw sand in the
economy's gears. It just needs to take its foot off the accelerator. We
wouldn't rule out a 50bps hike in December.
💡
Join the discussion with Dr. Ed below! Paid members can now

clause.

## Document 295: AI军备竞赛推高融资成本：美国科技企业融资溢价创2003年以来最高

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI军备竞赛推高融资成本：美国科技企业融资溢价创2003年以来最高.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美国投资级科技企业借款⼈的平均债券融资成本，相对于美国整体市场的溢价已达到 2003
年以来最⾼⽔平。分析指出，随着融资供给继续膨胀，美国科技债券利差可能进⼀步扩
⼤。美国科技企业正在尝试通过不同货币和市场分散庞⼤的资⾦需求。   巴黎银⾏预计，
2027 年欧元区投资级公司债券净供应量将增⻓ 83% 。
摘要：
AI 基础设施建设的融资需求持续膨胀，正在推美国⾼科技企业的债务融资成本。随着美国科技公
司竞相投⼊数千亿美元建设数据中⼼和算⼒基础设施，债券市场开始要求更⾼的⻛险补偿。
彭博汇编的数据显⽰，美国投资级科技企业借款⼈的平均债券融资成本，相对于美国整体市场
的溢价已达到 2003 年以来最⾼⽔平。
美国科技企业债券利差升⾄三年来⾼位，⽽整体投资级债券市场的利差仍相对平稳，科技⾏业
与其他发⾏⼈之间的融资成本差距进⼀步扩⼤。
美国科技巨头仍需要⼤量资⾦维持 AI 基础设施建设，但随着债券发⾏规模不断增加，投资者开始
要求更⾼收益率，以补偿潜在的信⽤⻛险和供给压⼒。分析指出，随着融资供给继续膨胀，美

国科技债券利差可能进⼀步扩⼤。
AI 融资成本⾼企，企业普遍尝试跨市场融资
数据中⼼、芯⽚和电⼒基础设施需要持续投⼊巨额资⾦，⾕歌、微软、 Meta 、亚⻢逊和甲⻣⽂
等⼤型科技公司正在通过债券市场为相关投资筹集资⾦。
美国科技企业正在尝试通过不同货币和市场分散庞⼤的资⾦需求。   巴黎银⾏预计， 2027 年欧元
区投资级公司债券净供应量将增⻓ 83% ，达到 1920 亿欧元（ 2140 亿美元），⾼于今年的 1050 亿
欧元。
不过，融资渠道增加并不意味着市场承接能⼒可以⽆限扩张。随着发⾏规模上升，投资者对新
增债券的⻛险补偿要求也在提⾼。
AI 融资⽅对融资价格相对不敏感，因为它们需要⼤量资⾦维持竞争优势。但当越来越多企业同时
进⼊债券市场，投资者的资⾦和⻛险承受能⼒终究存在边界，科技企业可能不得不提供更⾼收
益率，才能顺利完成融资。
甲⻣⽂等企业信⽤⻛险受到关注
债券市场对 AI 融资⻛险的重新定价，已经体现在部分⼤型科技企业的信⽤利差和信⽤违约互换
（ CDS ）市场中。
超⼤规模云服务商债券收益率近期上升，甲⻣⽂债券利差尤其受到关注。摩根⼤通分析师指

出，甲⻣⽂债券利差约为整体投资级债券指数的 2.8 倍，创历史最⾼⽐例。
AI 相关企业的信⽤⻛险对冲成本正在上升。Alphabet 的信⽤违约互换成本过去⼀年接近翻倍，
甲⻣⽂ CDS 报价也明显⾛⾼。

### Page 2

图⽚
据英国⾦融时报， AI 相关 CDS 市场交易活动正在增加，相关 CDS 净名义本⾦从⼀年前不⾜ 70 亿
美元升⾄接近 300 亿美元。英伟达五年期 CDS 利差升⾄ 141.5 个基点，反映投资者对科技⾏业债
务⻛险的关注正在扩⼤。
CDS 市场的变化并不意味着相关企业即将发⽣违约，但表明投资者正在增加⻛险对冲，并对 AI
产业链企业的偿债能⼒和未来融资需求保持更⾼警惕。
AI 投资回报与债务负担成为市场焦点
科技企业融资成本上升的背后，是 AI 基础设施投资规模与未来现⾦流回报之间的不确定性。
⼤型科技公司仍在加速建设数据中⼼和扩充算⼒，但相关投资需要持续投⼊资本，⽽投资回报
的兑现周期、 AI 业务盈利能⼒以及未来资本开⽀规模仍存在不确定性。
随着债务融资规模扩⼤，投资者不仅关注企业能否继续筹集资⾦，也开始更加重视其未来现⾦

流能否覆盖新增债务负担。

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy

trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm.

tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15)

Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

## Document 324: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-05)

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

spike feed acyclical inflation.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Exhibit 2: Charting the “Yentervention” and U.S. Treasury Yields Surge Across the Curve.
A) The Yen Regains Some Strength. B) 5-Year Forward Yields Hold Above 5%.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.
Exhibit 3: Pulse Check on: Equity/Bond Volatility and Diesel/Gasoline Prices.
A) Bond and Equity Market Volatility Diverge. B) Pain at the Pump: Diesel Prices Surge Ahead of Gasoline.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.
Exhibit 4: U.S. Exceptionalism: Record Inflows of Portfolio and FDI Inflows.
A) Net Foreign Purchases of US Equities and Investment Funds. B) FDI Inflows to the U.S.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.
Nonfinancial assets, such as closely held businesses, real estate, fine art, oil, gas and mineral properties, and timber, farm and ranch land, are complex in nature and involve risks including total
